Audio Adaptations — local SCP Wiki snapshot

Canon source comparison
Hosted SCP Wiki ↗ · Local JSON record · Canon source directory

This is the local archive snapshot, not a live copy. Open the hosted page alongside it to compare; no automatic equality or freshness claim is made.

Open original dataset JSON shard (find record key audio-adaptations; use the wiki server for HTTP access). Open exact single-record JSON.

The linked JSON is an unchanged single-record extraction from that dataset file. It is packaged here so it works when serving just the game wiki and when reading offline. The cloned archive is left untouched.

Archived article text

Escaped source text; archived scripts and HTML are never executed. Article statements are source fiction, not automatically adopted game mechanics.

<p><span style="font-size:larger;">Showcase for the best audio adaptations of articles on this wiki and on <a href="#wl">The Wanderers' Library</a>.</span></p>
<p><span style="white-space: pre-wrap;"> </span></p>
<div style="text-align: center;">
<div class="collapsible-block">
<div class="collapsible-block-folded"><a class="collapsible-block-link" href="javascript:;">+ How to add to this list</a></div>
<div class="collapsible-block-unfolded" style="display:none">
<div class="collapsible-block-unfolded-link"><a class="collapsible-block-link" href="javascript:;">- How to add to this list</a></div>
<div class="collapsible-block-content">
<div style="text-align: left;">
<ul>
<li>Adaptations linked here should be completely faithful to the source material. Links here should not lead to adaptations that substitute in new words in place of redactions, or adaptations that are missing supplemental pages, or adaptations that skip sections of the article, such as the Containment Procedures.</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>SCPs, tales, and GoI format adaptations are stored in separate pages. Changes made to those pages are reflected on this main page.<br>
Edit the following pages:
<ul>
<li><a href="/scp-series-10-audio-edition">SCP Series 10 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-9-audio-edition">SCP Series 9 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-8-audio-edition">SCP Series 8 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-7-audio-edition">SCP Series 7 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-6-audio-edition">SCP Series 6 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-5-audio-edition">SCP Series 5 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-4-audio-edition">SCP Series 4 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-3-audio-edition">SCP Series 3 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-2-audio-edition">SCP Series 2 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/scp-series-1-audio-edition">SCP Series 1 - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/foundation-tales-audio-edition">Foundation Tales - Audio Edition</a></li>
<li><a href="/goi-formats-audio-edition">GoI Formats - Audio Edition</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Links to adapations of other types of article are part of this page. So edit this page if you have an essay, hub, etc. adaptation to add.</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>If you see that a line you are adding to is commented out <tt><span style="white-space: pre-wrap;">[!--like this--]</span></tt>, remove the <tt><span style="white-space: pre-wrap;">[!--</span></tt> and <tt><span style="white-space: pre-wrap;">--]</span></tt> to un-comment and un-hide that line.</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>The names of the content producers/voice actors in the "Audio Links" column must be under 20 characters long.</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Please leave a descriptive [revision-note.png revision note].</li>
</ul>
<ul>
<li>Avoid adding text-to-speech adaptations unless they are of exceptionally high quality.</li>
</ul>
<p>To control bloat there is a limit of three adaptations per article. Feel free to add new readings in excess of this limit, but this page's maintainers will occasionally prune the lists down to three again.</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<p><span style="white-space: pre-wrap;"> </span></p>
<p>For original audio works (interviews, reviews and audio dramas), see the <a href="/podcast-hub">Podcast Hub</a>.</p>
